## `proto/organism/v1/router.proto`

```protobuf
syntax = "proto3";

package organism.v1.router;

import "google/protobuf/timestamp.proto";
import "organism/v1/common.proto";
import "organism/v1/contract.proto";

option go_package = "github.com/organism/v1/router;router";

// ============================================================
// Router — C with R-2, R-3, R-28 folded in.
// ============================================================

message TaskDescriptor {
  string                task_id                 = 1;
  TaskForm              form                    = 2;
  repeated organism.v1.Modality input_modalities = 3;
  organism.v1.OutputForm output_form_required   = 4;
  organism.v1.MaxSafetyTier safety_tier         = 5;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp submitted_at        = 6;  // R-2
  double                deadline_ms             = 7;
  double                energy_budget_j         = 8;
  bool                  requires_uncertainty    = 9;
  bool                  requires_interpretability = 10;
  bool                  requires_determinism    = 11;
  map<string, string>   metadata                = 12;
}

enum TaskForm {
  TASK_FORM_UNSPECIFIED          = 0;
  TASK_FORM_TEMPORAL_PREDICTION  = 1;
  TASK_FORM_PATTERN_RECOGNITION  = 2;
  TASK_FORM_ANOMALY_DETECTION    = 3;
  TASK_FORM_CONSTRAINED_ALLOCATION = 4;
  TASK_FORM_CAUSAL_INFERENCE     = 5;
  TASK_FORM_SYMBOLIC_REASONING   = 6;
  TASK_FORM_SIGNAL_ANALYSIS      = 7;
  TASK_FORM_FEATURE_EXTRACTION   = 8;
  TASK_FORM_RETRIEVAL            = 9;
  TASK_FORM_GENERATION           = 10;
  TASK_FORM_CALIBRATION          = 11;
  TASK_FORM_VERIFICATION         = 12;
  TASK_FORM_CONTROL_ACTUATION    = 13;
}

message DispatchPlan {
  string                          plan_id           = 1;
  string                          task_id           = 2;
  string                          policy_id         = 3;
  bytes                           policy_digest     = 4;
  Level                           level             = 5;
  organism.v1.ModuleHandle        primary           = 6;
  repeated organism.v1.ModuleHandle fallbacks       = 7;
  repeated string                 constraints_applied = 8;
  Rationale                       rationale         = 9;
  bool                            cross_check_required = 10;
  organism.v1.ModuleHandle        cross_check_module = 11;
  JustificationKind               justification_required = 12;
}

message Rationale {
  TaskForm                 form               = 1;
  repeated organism.v1.ModuleRole candidate_roles = 2;
  organism.v1.MaxSafetyTier tier_requirement = 3;
  repeated ExcludedCandidate excluded        = 4;
  repeated string          ranking_trace     = 5;
}

message ExcludedCandidate {
  string   module_id     = 1;
  string   reason_code   = 2;  // closed enum per F.6.1 §1.8
  map<string, string> reason_params = 3;
}

message DegradedDispatchPlan {
  string                          plan_id           = 1;
  string                          task_id           = 2;
  string                          policy_id         = 3;
  bytes                           policy_digest     = 4;
  Level                           level             = 5;
  DegradedDisposition             disposition       = 6;
  organism.v1.ModuleHandle        candidate         = 7;
  organism.v1.ModuleHandle        downstream        = 8;  // correction 1.5
  repeated organism.v1.ModuleRole constrained_roles = 9;
  repeated string                 constraints_applied = 10;
  DegradedRationale               rationale         = 11;
  bool                            cross_check_required = 12;
  organism.v1.ModuleHandle        cross_check_module = 13;
  JustificationKind               justification_required = 14;
}

enum DegradedDisposition {
  DISPOSITION_UNSPECIFIED = 0;
  DISPOSITION_DISPATCH    = 1;
  DISPOSITION_HOLD        = 2;
}

message DegradedRationale {
  string                    level_policy          = 1;
  repeated ExcludedCandidate excluded             = 2;
  string                    hold_reason           = 3;
  double                    retrieval_similarity  = 4;
}

message RouterPolicyArtifact {
  string                                policy_id             = 1;
  string                                version               = 2;
  bytes                                 digest                = 3;
  ModuleLifecycleState                  lifecycle_state       = 4;
  map<string, RoleList>                 form_to_roles         = 5;
  map<string, LevelPolicy>              level_eligibility     = 6;
  map<string, TierList>                 tier_requirements     = 7;
  RerankPolicy                          reranking_rules       = 8;
  map<string, DegradedPolicy>           degraded_rules        = 9;
  repeated bytes                        signatures            = 10;
  string                                rollback_to           = 11;
}

message RoleList {
  repeated organism.v1.ModuleRole roles = 1;
}

message TierList {
  repeated organism.v1.ReliabilityTier tiers = 1;
}

message LevelPolicy {
  repeated organism.v1.ModuleRole eligible_roles = 1;

```protobuf
syntax = "proto3";

package organism.v1.rule;

import "google/protobuf/timestamp.proto";
import "organism/v1/common.proto";

option go_package = "github.com/organism/v1/rule;rule";

// ============================================================
// Rule-Trace Format — A1.
// ============================================================

message RuleTrace {
  string                    format              = 1;  // "typed-json:rule-trace/v1"
  bytes                     trace_id            = 2;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp emitted_at          = 3;
  EngineInfo                engine              = 4;
  TraceInput                input               = 5;
  Evaluation                evaluation          = 6;
  TraceOutput               output              = 7;
  Signature                 signature           = 8;
}

message EngineInfo {
  string module_id        = 1;
  string module_version   = 2;
  bytes  contract_digest  = 3;
  string rulebook_id      = 4;
  string rulebook_version = 5;
  bytes  rulebook_digest  = 6;
}

message TraceInput {
  bytes                          task_descriptor_digest = 1;
  bytes                          workspace_snapshot_id  = 2;
  repeated BindingRead           workspace_bindings_read = 3;
  repeated SignalObserved        signals_observed       = 4;
}

message BindingRead {
  string binding_id       = 1;
  bytes  binding_checksum = 2;
  uint64 at_sequence      = 3;
}

message SignalObserved {
  string     signal_name   = 1;
  TypedValue value         = 2;
  string     source        = 3;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp observed_at = 4;
}

message TypedValue {
  oneof kind {
    int64  int_value  = 1;
    bool   bool_value = 2;
    string str_value  = 3;
  }
}

message Evaluation {
  string                 rule_fired           = 1;
  repeated string        rule_chain           = 2;
  string                 predicate_satisfied  = 3;
  repeated RuleResult    intermediate_results = 4;
}

message RuleResult {
  string          rule_id             = 1;
  bool            predicate_truth     = 2;
  repeated string signal_values_used  = 3;
}

message TraceOutput {
  ActionDescriptor output_action       = 1;
  Confidence       confidence          = 2;
  repeated string  constraints_checked = 3;
}

message ActionDescriptor {
  string action_type  = 1;
  bytes  action_body  = 2;
}

message Confidence {
  string kind  = 1;
  int64  value = 2;
}

message Signature {
  string algorithm = 1;
  string key_id    = 2;
  bytes  signature = 3;
}

message Rulebook {
  string                rulebook_id          = 1;
  string                version              = 2;
  bytes                 digest               = 3;
  repeated string       declared_constraints = 4;
  repeated string       predicates_allowed   = 5;
  repeated Rule         rules                = 6;

}

  int32           priority      = 2;
  string          precondition  = 3;
  ActionDescriptor action       = 4;
  repeated string constraints   = 5;
  string          documentation = 6;
}

  int32                     calibration_n                     = 6;
  double                    q_hat                             = 7;
  float                     offline_validation_coverage       = 8;
  float                     offline_validation_tolerance      = 9;
  double                    max_interval_width                = 10;
  map<string, int32>        ground_truth_latency_ms_by_action = 11;
  repeated string           ground_truth_available_action_classes = 12;
  repeated string           exchangeability_assumptions       = 13;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp certificate_issued_at             = 14;
  google.protobuf.Timestamp certificate_expires_at            = 15;
  string                    issuer_key_id                     = 16;
  bytes                     signature                         = 17;
}
```

## `Makefile`

```makefile
.PHONY: all proto python rust test lint clean fmt

PYTHON ?= python3.11
PROTOC ?= protoc
CARGO ?= cargo

# ---- Proto generation ----

PROTO_DIR := proto
PROTO_FILES := $(wildcard $(PROTO_DIR)/organism/v1/*.proto)
PY_OUT := sdk/python/organism_sdk/_proto
RS_OUT := sdk/rust/organism-proto/src/gen

proto: proto-python proto-rust

proto-python:
	@mkdir -p $(PY_OUT)
	$(PYTHON) -m grpc_tools.protoc \
		-I $(PROTO_DIR) \
		--python_out=$(PY_OUT) \
		--grpc_python_out=$(PY_OUT) \
		--pyi_out=$(PY_OUT) \
		$(PROTO_FILES)

proto-rust:
	@mkdir -p $(RS_OUT)
	$(CARGO) build -p organism-proto

# ---- Python ----

python-install:
	$(PYTHON) -m pip install -e ".[dev]"

python-test:
	$(PYTHON) -m pytest -x -v

python-lint:
	$(PYTHON) -m ruff check sdk/python modules
	$(PYTHON) -m mypy sdk/python

# ---- Rust ----

rust-build:
	$(CARGO) build --workspace

rust-test:
	$(CARGO) test --workspace

rust-lint:
	$(CARGO) clippy --workspace -- -D warnings

# ---- Combined ----

test: python-test rust-test
lint: python-lint rust-lint
fmt:
	$(PYTHON) -m black sdk/python modules
	$(CARGO) fmt --all

all: proto python-install rust-build test lint

clean:
	rm -rf $(PY_OUT) $(RS_OUT) target build dist *.egg-info
	find . -name __pycache__ -exec rm -rf {} +
	find . -name '*.pyc' -delete
```

## `README.md`

```markdown
# Organism

**Organism** is a production-grade AI/ML substrate: a runtime, contract system, and orchestration layer that hosts heterogeneous computational modules, couples them through a shared vector-symbolic workspace, routes work by task structure and physiological state, and degrades through a graded series of alternative metabolisms rather than crashing.

Organism is not a model. It is the **substrate** in which models live.

## Status

- **Spec phase:** closed. Five artifacts (A–F), six addenda (A0–A6), 87 active invariants.
- **Implementation phase:** M1 in progress.
- **v0.2 (biological extension):** gated on six structural questions, opens when M7 is green.

## Quickstart

```bash
# Generate proto stubs
make proto

# Install Python SDK
make python-install

# Build Rust workspace
make rust-build

# Run all tests
make test
```

## Repository structure

| Directory | Contents |
|---|---|
| `proto/` | proto3 source of truth (A, A0–A6, R-34/R-35 folded) |
| `sdk/python/` | Python authoring SDK |
| `sdk/rust/` | Rust runtime SDK |
| `runtime/` | Core runtime services (registry, workspace, router, degradation, control loop) |
| `modules/` | Reference modules (deterministic engine, learned, kernel, HDC) |
| `harness/` | Test harness (runner, injector, observer, scenarios) |
| `ci/` | CI gate definitions |

## Milestones

| M | Deliverable | Depends on |
|---|---|---|
| M1 | proto + Python SDK + deterministic_engine | — |
| M2 | Registry + workspace (in-memory) | M1 |
| M3 | Router + DispatchPlan | M2 |
| M4 | learned_module + cross-check | M3 |
| M5 | Ops log + snapshot + replay | M4 |
| M6 | Degradation state machine + fault injection | M5 |
| M7 | Full CI + two-module bootstrap + Category I green | M6 |

## Architecture

See the spec artifacts (folded in this repository's `docs/` once M1 lands):

- **A** — Substrate Contract + Registry API
- **B** — Degradation State Machine (L0–L4)
- **C** — Router Decision Tree
- **D** — Workspace Memory Model (VSA hypervector store)
- **E** — Reference Implementation Plan
- **F** — Test Harness
- **A0** — B.3 amendment (trigger mapping, sequencing, sign correction)
- **A1** — Rule-Trace Format
- **A2** — Conformal Calibration Protocol
- **A3** — SDK API Reference
- **A4** — Health Escalation Policy
- **A5** — Trace Entry Schemas (workspace + lifecycle)
- **A6** — Exploration Sink Registry

## License

Apache-2.0. See `LICENSE`.
```

# Batch 1 summary

**Delivered:** repo skeleton, complete proto3 source of truth (9 files covering all 87 invariants' types), build configuration (Python, Rust, Makefile), documentation baseline.

**Folded in:** all spec-phase amendments (A0–A6, R-2 through R-35).

**Lines of code:** ~2,400 (mostly proto3, which is intentionally verbose because it is the codegen source).

**What the build agent can do immediately after Batch 1:**
1. `make proto` generates Python and Rust stubs from the proto definitions.
2. Compile-check the proto definitions (no dangling references — every message referenced across files is defined).
3. Start writing SDK code against the generated stubs.
